CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
主席報告

BUSINESS REVIEW                                                                      業務回顧
On behalf of the board (“Board”) of directors (“Directors”), I am pleased to         本人謹代表董事會，欣然向各位股東公佈，建
report to our shareholders that Kingboard Laminates Holdings Limited and its         滔積層板控股有限公司及其附屬公司（「集團」）
subsidiaries (the “Group”) achieved satisfactory results for the financial year      於截至二零一九年十二月三十一日止財政年度
ended 31 December 2019. During the year, demand increased continuously               取得理想業績。二零一九年，5G通訊等新興電
in some emerging sectors, such as those in relation to 5G communications.            子市場需求持續增長，惟未能完全抵銷中美貿
However, this growth was not sufficient to offset the impacts that the Sino-         易摩擦對市場造成的影響。集團管理層憑藉敏
US trade friction had on the market. With the management’s astute market             銳的市場觸覺，一方面以積極的定價策略，加
intelligence, the Group’s laminates segment has been able to reinforce               大原有客戶的滲透，另一方面全力提高包括薄
penetration into existing customers through an active pricing strategy, while        板、高頻高速覆銅面板及無鹵素等高階、高附
on the other hand it seeks to enhance the market shares of advanced and              加值產品的份額，拓展新的市場領域。在二零
high value-adding products such as thin, high-frequency and high-speed and           一九年下半年終收穫顯著成效，業務表現強力
halogen-free laminates, supporting us in our venture into new market areas.          反彈。覆銅面板銷量進入持續上升通道，盈利
With this two-pronged strategy, the Group was able to achieve remarkable             水平亦隨之提升。覆銅面板部門憑藉完善的垂
results and a strong rebound in the second half of 2019. As laminates sales          直供應產業鏈和高效的執行力，繼續保持全球
entered on a growth track, profitability of the segment has improved. By             領先市場地位。
virtue of an immaculate vertical supply chain and operational efficiency, the
laminates segment continued to maintain its status in the market as a global
leader.

the latter part of 2019. The Group’s upstream plants were able to maintain         一九年下半年的強勁勢頭，集團上游物料廠房

work and have gradually restored to full production capacity. Apart from           全面復工，並逐步恢復至滿負荷生產狀態。集
striving for work resumption, the Group also emphasises standing by its            團除全力推動復產外，亦強調堅守企業社會責
corporate social responsibilities. To this end, the Group has donated supplies     任。除多次向受疫情影響地區作出物資捐贈
to infection-stricken areas, and has enforced stringent preventive measures        外，於工廠範圍內亦實行嚴格的防疫措施，成
within its factories in a successful attempt to avoid infection among its          功避免員工受到感染。
employees.

We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Kingboard                  本核數師（以下簡稱「我們」）已審計列載於第69

summary of significant accounting policies.

of most significance in our audit of the consolidated financial statements           本期綜合財務報表的審計最為重要的事項。這

 Assessment of net realisable values of properties held for development (“PHD”)

 their net realisable value and the future costs to completion    •      Evaluating the reasonableness of the costs recognised

 我們將評審待發展物業的可變現淨值識別為關鍵審計事                                         我們評審待發展物業的可變現淨值的程序包括：
 項，此乃由於估計其可變現淨值和完成待發展物業未來費
 用涉及重大金額及判斷。                                                      •      透過獲取獨立合資格測量師就已產生的成本及完成進度的
                                                                         直接確認，抽樣評估待發展物業確認的成本合理性；
 於二零一九年十二月三十一日， 貴集團擁有位於中華人
 民共和國（「中國」）的待發展物業賬面值為17億港元。                                       •      按照我們對有關地點物業市場的認識，透過將待發展物業
                                                                         與同一項目或相關地點類似物業的近期交易價作比較，抽
 誠如綜合財務報表附註4及25所載，待發展物業的可變現                                              樣評審待發展物業的估計日後售價的適當性；及
 淨值參考估計日後售價所釐定。日後售價參考同一項目或
 相關地點類似物業的近期售價估計。此外，管理層參考已                                        •     基於已產生的實際開發成本及 貴集團物業的完工進度
 產生的實際發展成本及完成進度，以估計完成待發展物業                                              （連同有關調整），再計及現行市場數據及類似物業之歷史
 的未來費用。                                                                 記錄，抽樣評估完成待發展物業的估計日後成本的合理
                                                                        性。

             the consolidated financial statements of the Group for the                   款，於過渡時已調整至反映貼現
             current year.                                                                效應。貼現效應對本集團本年度
                                                                                          的綜合財務申報並無重大影響。



     There is no impact of transition to HKFRS 16 on retained profits as at           於二零一九年一月一日，過渡至香港財

            information;

            simply referring to “users” which was considered too broad                  非僅指「使用者」，於決定於財務
            when deciding what information to disclose in the financial                 報表披露何等資料時，「使用者」
            statements.                                                                 一詞被視為過於廣義。

     The amendments also align the definition across all HKFRSs and will            該等修訂與各香港財務申報準則的定義

     to have significant impact on the financial position and performance           用該等修訂不會對本集團的財務狀況及
     of the Group but may affect the presentation and disclosures in the            表現造成重大影響，惟可能影響於財務
     consolidated financial statements.                                             報表中的呈列及披露。

3.   SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                             3.   主要會計政策
     The consolidated financial statements have been prepared in                      綜合財務報表乃按香港會計師公會頒佈
     accordance with HKFRSs issued by the HKICPA. In addition, the                    的香港財務申報準則編製。此外，綜合
     consolidated financial statements include applicable disclosures                 財務報表載有聯交所證券上市規則（「上
     required by the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on the                 市規則」）及香港公司條例之適用披露規
     Stock Exchange (“Listing Rules”) and by the Hong Kong Companies                  定。
     Ordinance.

     The consolidated financial statements have been prepared on the                  綜合財務報表乃按歷史成本基準編製，
     historical cost basis except for investment properties and certain               惟如下文所載之會計政策所闡述，投資
     financial instruments, that are measured at fair values at the end of            物業及若干金融工具則於各報告期間結
     each reporting period, as explained in the accounting policies set out           束當日按公平值計量。
     below.

     Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the consideration        歷史成本一般按為換取貨品及服務而付
     given in exchange for goods and services.                                        出的代價的公平值計算。

     Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or               公平值是於計量日期市場參與者間於有
     paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between market            秩序交易中出售資產所收取或轉讓負債
     participants at the measurement date, regardless of whether that                 須支付之價格，而不論該價格為可直接
     price is directly observable or estimated using another valuation                觀察取得或可使用其他估值方法估計。
     technique. In estimating the fair value of an asset or a liability, the          於估計資產或負債之公平值時，本集團
     Group takes into account the characteristics of the asset or liability           會考慮該等市場參與者於計量日期對資
     if market participants would take those characteristics into account             產或負債定價時所考慮之資產或負債之
     when pricing the asset or liability at the measurement date. Fair value          特點。於該等綜合財務報表中作計量
     for measurement and/or disclosure purposes in these consolidated                 及╱或披露用途之公平值乃按此基準釐
     financial statements is determined on such a basis, except for share-            定，惟以下各項除外：屬於香港財務申
     based payment transactions that are within the scope of HKFRS 2                  報準則第2號「以股份形式付款」範圍內
     Share-based Payment, leasing transactions that are accounted for                 之以股份形式付款交易、按照香港財務
     in accordance with HKFRS 16 (since 1 January 2019) or HKAS 17                    申報準則第16號（自二零一九年一月一
     (before application of HKFRS 16), and measurements that have some                日起）或香港會計準則第17號（應用香港
     similarities to fair value but are not fair value, such as net realisable        財務申報準則第16號前）入賬之租賃交
     value in HKAS 2 Inventories or value in use in HKAS 36 Impairment of             易，以及與公平值存在某些相似之處但
     Assets.                                                                          並非公平值之項目計量，例如香港會計
                                                                                      準則第2號「存貨」之可變現淨值或香港

3.   SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                                 3.   主要會計政策（續）
     (continued)

     A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into account                 非金融資產公平值之計量則參考市場參
     a market participant’s ability to generate economic benefits by using                與者可從使用該資產得到之最高及最佳
     the asset in its highest and best use or by selling it to another market             效用，或把該資產售予另一可從使用該
     participant that would use the asset in its highest and best use.                    資產得到最高及最佳效用之市場參與者
                                                                                          所產生之經濟效益。

     For financial instruments and investment properties which are                        就其後期間使用不可觀察輸入數據按公
     transacted at fair value and a valuation technique that unobservable                 平值計量的金融工具及投資物業，估值
     inputs is to be used to measure fair values in subsequent periods,                   方法已經校準，使估值方法的結果與交
     the valuation technique is calibrated so that at initial recognition the             易價格相等。
     results of the valuation technique equal the transaction price.

     In addition, for financial reporting purposes, fair value measurements               此外，就財務申報而言，公平值計量分
     are categorised into Level 1, 2 or 3 based on the degree to which                    為第一級別、第二級別或第三級別，此
     the inputs to the fair value measurements are observable and the                     等級別之劃分乃根據公平值計量輸入數
     significance of the inputs to the fair value measurement in its entirety,            據的可觀察程度及該輸入數據對公平值
     which are described as follows:                                                      計量的整體重要性，概述如下：

     •       Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets              •    第一級別輸入數據指該實體於計
             for identical assets or liabilities that the entity can access at                 量日期由活躍市場上相同資產或
             the measurement date;                                                             負債獲得的報價（未經調整）；

     •       Level 2 inputs are inputs, other than quoted prices included                 •    第二級別輸入數據指除第一級別
             within Level 1, that are observable for the asset or liability,                   所包含之報價以外，資產或負債
             either directly or indirectly; and                                                可直接或間接觀察得出的數據；
                                                                                               及

     •       Level 3 inputs are unobservable inputs for the asset or liability.           •    第三級別輸入數據指資產或負債
                                                                                               的不可觀察數據。

3.   SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                              3.   主要會計政策（續）
     (continued)


     Basis of consolidation                                                            綜合賬目基準
     The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial                   綜合財務報表包括本公司及由本公司及
     statements of the Company and entities controlled by the Company                  其附屬公司控制之實體之財務報表。當
     and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved when the Company:                       本公司符合以下情況，即取得控制權：

     •       has power over the investee;                                              •   有權控制被投資方；

     •       is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its                   •   因其參與被投資方業務而對可變
             involvement with the investee; and                                            回報承擔風險或享有權利；及

     •       has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.                   •   有能力以其權力影響其回報。

     The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if facts              倘有事實及情況顯示上列三項控制權條
     and circumstances indicate that there are changes to one or more of               件之其中一項或多項有變，本集團會重
     the three elements of control listed above.                                       新評估其是否控制被投資方。

     Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group obtains control               當本集團取得附屬公司之控制權，便將
     over the subsidiary and ceases when the Group loses control of the                該附屬公司綜合入賬；當本集團失去附
     subsidiary. Specifically, income and expenses of a subsidiary acquired            屬公司之控制權，便停止將該附屬公司
     or disposed of during the year are included in the consolidated                   綜合入賬。具體而言，年內收購或出售
     statement of profit or loss from the date the Group gains control until           之附屬公司之收入及支出會於本集團取
     the date when the Group ceases to control the subsidiary.                         得控制權當日起計入綜合損益表，直至
                                                                                       本集團對該附屬公司之控制權終止當日
                                                                                       為止。

     Profit or loss and each item of other comprehensive income are                    損益及其他全面收益的各個項目乃歸屬

     An item of properties, plant and equipment is derecognised upon                  物業、廠房及設備項目於出售或預期持
     disposal or when no future economic benefits are expected to arise               續使用該資產不會產生未來經濟利益時
     from the continued use of the asset. Any gain or loss arising on the             剔除確認。出售或報廢物業、廠房及設
     disposal or retirement of an item of properties, plant and equipment             備項目所產生的任何盈虧為該資產出售
     is determined as the difference between the sales proceeds and the               所得款項與賬面值之間的差額，該差額
     carrying amount of the asset and is recognised in profit or loss.                在損益中確認。

     Licenced properties, plant and equipment                                         授權使用物業、廠房及設備
     Licenced properties, plant and equipment (included in properties,                持作賺取授權使用費的授權使用物業、
     plant and equipment) held to earn licence fee income are stated in the           廠房及設備（計入物業、廠房及設備），

     accumulated depreciation and any accumulated impairment losses, if               值虧損（如有）在綜合財務狀況表列賬。
     any.

     Depreciation is recognised as to write off the cost of items of licenced         授權使用物業、廠房及設備項目之折舊
     properties, plant and equipment less their residual values over their            乃根據其估計可使用年期，於扣減其剩
     estimated useful lives using the straight-line method. The estimated             餘價值後，以直線法撇銷其成本。估計
     useful lives, residual values and depreciation method are reviewed               可使用年期、剩餘價值及折舊方法於各
     at the end of each reporting period, with the effect of any changes in           報告期間結束當日予以檢討，估計如有
     estimate accounted for on a prospective basis.                                   任何變動，其影響在日後入賬。

     Inventories                                                                      存貨
     Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net realisable value.            存貨按成本或可變現淨值兩者中之較低
     Cost of inventories are determined on a weighted average method.                 者入賬。存貨成本按加權平均法計算。
     Net realisable value represents the estimated selling price for                  可變現淨值指存貨估計售價減估計完成
     inventories less all estimated costs of completion and costs necessary           所需之全部成本以及銷售所需之成本。
     to make the sale.

      Taxation                                                                            稅項
      Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently payable                  所得稅開支指現時應繳稅項及遞延稅項
      and deferred tax.                                                                   之總和。

      The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the year.                  現時應繳稅項乃按本年度應課稅溢利計
      Taxable profit differs from profit before tax because of income or                  算。基於其他年度應課稅或可扣稅的收
      expense that are taxable or deductible in other years and items that                入或開支項目，以及毋須課稅或扣稅項
      are never taxable or deductible. The Group’s liability for current tax              目，因此應課稅溢利與除稅前溢利不
      is calculated using tax rates that have been enacted or substantively               同。本集團即期稅項負債乃按報告期間
      enacted by the end of the reporting period.                                         結束當日已頒佈或實際已頒佈之稅率計
                                                                                          算。

      Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences between the                     遞延稅項乃根據綜合財務報表資產及負
      carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the consolidated financial            債賬面值與計算應課稅溢利所採用相應
      statements and the corresponding tax bases used in the computation                  稅基間之臨時差額確認。遞延稅項負債
      of taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities are generally recognised for            一般會就所有應課稅臨時差額確認，而
      all taxable temporary differences. Deferred tax assets are generally                遞延稅項資產一般在可能出現可利用臨
      recognised for all deductible temporary difference to the extent that it is         時差額扣稅之應課稅溢利時就所有可扣
      probable that taxable profits will be available against which deductible            減臨時差額確認。因業務合併以外交易
      temporary differences can be utilised. Such deferred tax assets and                 初步確認資產及負債且不影響應課稅溢
      liabilities are not recognised if the temporary difference arises from the          利亦不影響會計溢利而引致之臨時差
      initial recognition (other than in a business combination) of assets and            額，則不會確認該等遞延稅項資產及負
      liabilities in a transaction that affects neither the taxable profit nor the        債。此外，不會就因初步確認商譽而引
      accounting profit. In addition, deferred tax liabilities are not recognised         致之臨時差額確認遞延稅項負債。
      if the temporary difference arises from the initial recognition of
      goodwill.

      Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable temporary                       遞延稅項負債乃按與於附屬公司之投資
      differences associated with investments in subsidiaries, except where               有關之應課稅臨時差額而確認，惟若本
      the Group is able to control the reversal of the temporary difference               集團可控制臨時差額之撥回及臨時差額
      and it is probable that the temporary difference will not reverse in                有可能不會於可見將來撥回之情況則除
      the foreseeable future. Deferred tax assets arising from deductible                 外。與該等投資及權益相關的可扣減臨
      temporary differences associated with such investments and interests                時差額所產生的遞延稅項資產，僅在可
      are only recognised to the extent that it is probable that there will               能出現可利用臨時差額扣稅之足夠應課
      be sufficient taxable profits against which to utilise the benefits of              稅溢利時，並預期於可見將來回撥時確
      the temporary differences and they are expected to reverse in the                   認。
      foreseeable future.

      Taxation (continued)                                                                 稅項（續）
      The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed at the end of                 遞延稅項資產之賬面值於各報告期間結
      each reporting period and reduced to the extent that it is no longer                 束當日作檢討，並在沒可能會有足夠應
      probable that sufficient taxable profits will be available to allow all or           課稅溢利收回全部或部分資產時加以遞
      part of the asset to be recovered.                                                   減。

      Deferred tax assets and liabilities are measured at the tax rates that               根據報告期間結束當日已頒佈或實際已
      are expected to apply in the period in which the liability is settled or             頒佈之稅率（及稅法），遞延稅項資產及
      the asset is realised, based on tax rate (and tax laws) that have been               負債按負債清償或資產變現期間預期適
      enacted or substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period.                 用之稅率計量。

      The measurement of deferred tax liabilities and assets reflects the tax              遞延稅項負債及資產之計量反映本集團
      consequences that would follow from the manner in which the Group                    預期於報告期間結束當日將出現的稅
      expects, at the end of the reporting period, to recover or settle the                務後果，以收回資產或清償負債之賬面
      carrying amount of its assets and liabilities.                                       值。

      For the purposes of measuring deferred tax for investment properties                 就計量以公平值模式計量投資物業之遞
      that are measured using the fair value model, the carrying amounts                   延稅項而言，假定該等物業之賬面值可
      of such properties are presumed to be recovered entirely through                     從出售中全數收回，除非假定被駁回則
      sale, unless the presumption is rebutted. The presumption is rebutted                另作別論。當投資物業可予折舊，並按
      when the investment property is depreciable and is held within a                     業務模式持有該等物業，其目的是隨時
      business model whose objective is to consume substantially all of the                間耗用投資物業所包含的絕大部分經濟
      economic benefits embodied in the investment property over time,                     利益（而並非透過出售），該假定被駁
      rather than through sale.                                                            回。

      For the purposes of measuring deferred tax for leasing transactions                  就本集團確認使用權資產及相關租賃負
      in which the Group recognises the right-of-use assets and the related                債的租賃交易計量遞延稅項而言，本集
      lease liabilities, the Group first determines whether the tax deductions             團首先釐定使用權資產或租賃負債是否
      are attributable to the right-of-use assets or the lease liabilities.                應佔稅項扣減。

      For leasing transactions in which the tax deductions are attributable                就稅項扣減歸屬於租賃負債之租賃交易
      to the lease liabilities, the Group applies HKAS 12 Income Taxes                     而言，本集團會對使用權資產及租賃負
      requirements to right-of-use assets and lease liabilities separately.                債分開應用香港會計準則第12號「所得
      Temporary differences on initial recognition of the relevant right-of-               稅 」之規定。由於應用初始確認豁免，
      use assets and lease liabilities are not recognised due to application               故不會確認有關使用權資產及租賃負債
      of the initial recognition exemption. Temporary differences arising                  於初始確認時之暫時差額。其後修訂使
      from subsequent revision to the carrying amounts of right-of-use                     用權資產及租賃負債賬面值、重新計量
      assets and lease liabilities, resulting from remeasurement of lease                  租賃負債及租賃修訂產生的暫時差額
      liabilities and lease modifications, that are not subject to initial                 （不適用於初始確認豁免），於重新計量

      or modification.

      The effective interest method is a method of calculating the amortised                 實際利率法是一種計算金融資產或金融
      cost of a financial asset or financial liability and of allocating interest            負債攤銷成本與相關期間分派利息收入
      income and interest expense over the relevant period. The effective                    及利息支出之方法。實際利率是於金融
      interest rate is the rate that exactly discounts estimated future cash                 資產或金融負債的估計可使用年期或
      receipts and payments (including all fees and points paid or received                  （如適用）更短期間將估計未來現金收入
      that form an integral part of the effective interest rate, transaction                 及付款（包括所有已付或已收並構成實
      costs and other premiums or discounts) through the expected life of                    際利率一部分之費用、交易成本及其他
      the financial asset or financial liability, or, where appropriate, a shorter           溢價或折價）精確折現至初步確認時之
      period, to the net carrying amount on initial recognition.                             賬面淨值之比率。

      Interest income from debt instruments at fair value through other                      於本集團日常業務過程中來自按公平值
      comprehensive income (“FVTOCI”) and dividend income from equity                        計入其他全面收益的債務工具的利息收
      instruments which are derived from the Group’s ordinary course                         入及來自權益工具的股息收入呈列為營
      of business are presented as revenue. Interest income from bank                        業額。銀行結餘及存款利息收入計入其
      balances and deposits are included in other income, gains and                          他收入、收益及虧損。
      losses.

      The Group performs impairment assessment under expected credit                  本集團根據預期信貸虧損模式對金融資

                                                                                      起的信貸風險變動。

      Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from all possible              全期預期信貸虧損指將因相關工具預計
      default events over the expected life of the relevant instrument. In            存續期內所有可能違約事件而導致的預
      contrast, 12m ECL represents the portion of lifetime ECL that is                期信貸虧損。12個月預期信貸虧損指預
      expected to result from default events that are possible within 12              計因報告日期後12個月內可能出現的違
      months after the reporting date. Assessments are done based on                  約事件導致的全期預期信貸虧損的一部
      the Group’s historical credit loss experience, adjusted for factors             分。評估基於本集團的過往信用損失經
      that are specific to the debtors, general economic conditions and an            驗作出，並就債務人特定因素、整體經
      assessment of both the current conditions at the reporting date as              濟狀況及報告日期現行狀況的評估及未
      well as the forecast of future conditions.                                      來狀況預測而作出調整。

      a provision matrix with appropriate groupings.                                  進行單項評估及╱或使用適當分組的撥

      ECL. The assessment of whether lifetime ECL should be recognised                集團確認全期預期信貸虧損。對是否應

      (i)    Significant increase in credit risk                                          (i) 信用風險顯著增加
             In assessing whether the credit risk has increased significantly                 在評估自初始確認起信用風險是
             since initial recognition, the Group compares the risk of a                      否顯著增加時，本集團將金融工
             default occurring on the financial instrument as at the reporting                具於報告日發生違約的風險與金
             date with the risk of a default occurring on the financial                       融工具於初始確認日期發生違約
             instrument as at the date of initial recognition. In making                      的風險進行比較。在作出本評估
             this assessment, the Group considers both quantitative and                       時，本集團考慮合理並有支持的
             qualitative information that is reasonable and supportable,                      定量及定性資料，包括過往經驗
             including historical experience and forward-looking information                  及無需付出不必要的額外成本或
             that is available without undue cost or effort.                                  努力即可獲得的前瞻性信息。

             In particular, the following information is taken into account                    具體而言，在評估信用風險有否
             when assessing whether credit risk has increased significantly:                   顯著增加時考慮以下信息：

             •       an actual or expected significant deterioration in the                     •    金融工具外部（如有）或
                     financial instrument’s external (if available) or internal                      內部信貸評級的實際或
                     credit rating;                                                                  預期的顯著轉差；

             •       significant deterioration in external market indicators                    •    信貸風險的外部市場指
                     of credit risk, e.g. a significant increase in the credit                       數顯著轉差，如信貸息
                     spread, the credit default swap prices for the debtor;                          差大幅上升、債務人信

      (iii)  Credit-impaired financial assets                                               (iii) 信貸減值金融資產
             A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or more events                         當發生一項或多項對金融資產估
             that have a detrimental impact on the estimated future cash                          計未來現金流量有不利影響之事
             flows of that financial asset have occurred. Evidence that a                         件時，金融資產出現信貸減值。
             financial asset is credit-impaired includes observable data                          金融資產信貸減值之證據包括以
             about the following events:                                                          下可觀察事件：

             (a)     significant financial difficulty of the issuer or the                         (a)   發行人或借款人發生重
                     borrower;                                                                           大財務困難；

             (b)     a breach of contract, such as a default or past due                           (b)   違反合約，如違約或逾
                     event;                                                                              期事件；

             (c)     the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or                                (c)   借款人之放款人因與借
                     contractual reasons relating to the borrower’s                                      款人有關之經濟或合約
                     financial difficulty, having granted to the borrower a                              原因，向借款人授出放
                     concession(s) that the lender(s) would not otherwise                                款人在其他情況下不會
                     consider; or                                                                        考慮授出之優惠；或

             (d)     it is becoming probable that the borrower will enter                          (d)   債務人很可能破產或進
                     bankruptcy or other financial reorganisation.                                       行其他財務重組。

      (iv)   Write-off policy                                                               (iv)   撇銷政策
             The Group writes off a financial asset when there is                                  倘有資料顯示對手方面對嚴重財
             information indicating that the counterparty is in severe                             務困難，且實際上並無收回款項
             financial difficulty and there is no realistic prospect of recovery,                  的前景（如對手方被清盤或進入
             for example, when the counterparty has been placed under                              破程訴訟），本集團會撇銷金融
             liquidation or has entered into bankruptcy proceedings.                               資產。於適當時，在考慮法律意
             Financial assets written off may still be subject to enforcement                      見後，本集團可能仍會根據收款
             activities under the Group’s recovery procedures, taking                              程序處理被撇銷的金融資產。撇
             into account legal advice where appropriate. A write-off                              銷構成取消確認事項。隨後收回
             constitutes a derecognition event. Any subsequent recoveries                          的任何款項於損益中確認。
             are recognised in profit or loss.

      (v)    Measurement and recognition of ECL                                      (v) 預期信貸虧損的計量及確認
             The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability of                  預期信貸虧損的計量取決於違約
             default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the loss if              概率、違約損失率（即如存在違
             there is a default) and the exposure at default. The assessment             約時的損失程度）及違約風險。
             of the probability of default and loss given default is based               對違約概率及違約損失率的評估
             on historical data adjusted by forward-looking information.                 基於歷史數據，並就前瞻性信息
             Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased and probability-                     作出調整。預期信貸虧損的估計
             weighted amount that is determined with the respective risks                反映以發生相關違約的風險為權
             of default occurring as the weights.                                        重的無偏概率加權金額。

             Generally, the ECL is the difference between all contractual               一般而言，預期信貸虧損為本集
             cash flows that are due to the Group in accordance with                    團按初始確認時釐定的實際利率
             the contract and the cash flows that the Group expects to                  貼現，按照合約應收到的所有合
             receive, discounted at the effective interest rate determined at           約現金流量與本集團預計收到的
             initial recognition.                                                       所有現金流量之間的差額。

             Where ECL is measured on a collective basis, the financial                 倘預期信貸虧損按集體基準計
             instruments are grouped on the basis below:                                量，則金融工具按以下基準歸
                                                                                        類：

             •       Nature of financial instruments (i.e. the Group’s trade            •   金融工具的性質（即本集
                     receivables, debt instruments at FVTOCI, amounts                       團貿易應收賬款，按公
                     due from fellow subsidiaries, bills receivables, bank                  平值計入其他全面收益
                     balances and other receivables, are each assessed as                   之債務工具，應收同系
                     separate groups);                                                      附屬公司款項，應收票
                                                                                            據，銀行結餘及其他應
                                                                                            收賬款按個別組別進行
                                                                                            評核）；

             •       Past-due status;                                                   •   逾期狀況；

             •       Nature, size and industry of debtors; and                          •   債務人的性質、規模及
                                                                                            行業；及

             •       External credit ratings where available.                           •   外部信貸評級（如有）
                                                                                                     。

             The grouping is regularly reviewed by management to ensure                 歸類工作經管理層定期檢討，以
             the constituents of each group continue to share similar credit            確保各組別成分繼續分擔類似信
             risk characteristics.                                                      貸風險特性。

             Interest income is calculated based on the gross carrying                  利息收入按金融資產的賬面總值
             amount of the financial asset unless the financial asset is                計算，除非金融資產出現信貸減

             based on amortised cost of the financial asset.                            金融資產的攤銷成本計算。

      and liabilities that are not readily apparent from other sources. The               斷、估計及假設。該等估計及相關假設

      period, or in the period of the revision and future periods if revision             則在修改期間及未來期間均會確認。
      affects both current and future periods.

      For the purposes of measuring deferred tax liabilities or deferred tax          就計量利用公平值模式的投資物業所產

6.    SEGMENT INFORMATION                                                             6.   分部資料
      HKFRS 8 “Operating Segments” requires operating segments to                          香港財務申報準則第8號「經營分部」要
      be identified on the basis of internal reports about components of                   求以本集團之主要營運決策者（「主要營
      the Group that are regularly reviewed by Chief Operating Decision                    運決策者」）在對分部作資源分配及評估
      Maker (“CODM”) in order to allocate resources to segments and                        其表現上所定期審閱的有關本集團不同
      to assess their performance. Specifically, the Group’s reportable                    部門之內部報告作為確定經營分部之基
      segments under HKFRS 8 are organised into three main operating                       準。具體而言，於香港財務申報準則第
      divisions – (i) manufacturing and sale of laminates; (ii) properties; and            8號下，本集團之須予呈報之分部分為
      (iii) investments. The management aggregated the sales of properties,                三個主要經營分部－(i)製造及銷售覆銅
      rental and hotel income business into one reportable segment                         面板(ii)物業及(iii)投資。管理層將物業銷
      because the financial performance of these businesses are affected                   售，物業出租及酒店收入業務合併為一
      by changes in the property market.                                                   個可報告分部，因為這些業務的財務表
                                                                                           現受到物業市場變動的影響。

      The accounting policies the Group used for segment reporting under                   本集團根據香港財務申報準則第8號用
      HKFRS 8 are the same as those used in its HKFRS consolidated                         作分部呈報之會計政策，與其用於香港
      financial statements. Segment profit or loss represents the profit                   財務申報準則之綜合財務報表一致。
      earned by each segment with certain items not included (unallocated                  分部損益代表各分部賺取的利潤，但不
      corporate income and expenses, share-based payments and finance                      包括某些項目（未分配的公司收入和支
      costs). This is the measure reported to the CODM for the purpose of                  出，以股份形式付款和融資成本），這
      resource allocation and performance assessment.                                      是報告給主要營運決策者用於資源分配
                                                                                           和績效評估的措施。

       PRC Enterprise Income Tax                           中國企業所得稅                            698,907       710,216
       PRC LAT                                             中國土地增值稅                            207,568       507,887
       Hong Kong Profits Tax                               香港利得稅                                3,842         1,251
       Taxation arising in other jurisdiction              其他司法權區之稅項                            2,139         2,465


                                                                                              912,456      1,221,819
       Deferred taxation                                   遞延稅項
         Credit for the year (Note 21)                      本年度撥回（附註21）                        (3,966)        (3,975)


                                                                                              908,490      1,217,844


      Under the Law of the People’s Republic of China on Enterprise                     按照中華人民共和國企業所得稅法（「企
      Income Tax (the “EIT Law”) and Implementation Regulation of the EIT               業所得稅法」）及其實施條例，由二零零
      Law, the tax rate of the PRC subsidiaries is 25% from 1 January 2008              八年一月一日開始，適用於中國附屬公
      onwards.                                                                          司之企業所得稅率為25%。

      The provision of LAT is estimated according to the requirements                   計提土地增值稅撥備是按有關中國稅
      set forth in the relevant PRC tax laws and regulations. LAT has                   法及規則要求而估算。按土地增值金
      been levied at progressive rates ranging from 30% to 60% on the                   額（根據物業銷售收入扣去指定直接成

      In determining the fair value of the relevant properties, the Directors            為釐定相關物業之公平值，董事設立估
      has set up a valuation committee, which is headed up by the                        值委員會，並由本集團之財務事宜負責
      persons in charge of financial matters of the Group to determine                   人領導，以釐定公平值計量之適當估值
      the appropriate valuation techniques and inputs for fair value                     技術及輸入數據。
      measurements.

      The Group engages an independent valuer to perform its valuations.                 本集團委聘獨立估值師估值。估值委員
      The valuation committee works closely with the independent valuer                  會與獨立估值師緊密合作，共同制定模
      to establish the appropriate valuation techniques and inputs to the                型之適當估值技術及輸入數據。財務事
      model. The persons in charge of financial matters report the valuation             宜負責人每季向董事匯報估值委員會之
      committee’s findings to the Directors every quarter to explain the                 發現，闡明資產及負債公平值波動之因
      cause of fluctuations in the fair value of the assets and liabilities.             由。

      The Group’s investment properties were classified as Level 3 of the                本集團投資物業於二零一九年及二零

      was required to replace and return the quantities of the licenced                  授權使用期結束時，獲授權人須向授權
      inventories used, consumed or disposed of during the licenced period               人重置及退還於授權使用期間已使用、
      to the Licensor at the end of the licence period.                                  耗用或出售的授權使用存貨數量。

      licenced period were secured by cash and bills receivables of a                    權使用存貨由獲授權人一名關聯方之現
      related party of the Licencee, with a total receivable value of not                金及應收票據作抵押，可收回總額不少
      less than the value of the licenced inventories used, consumed or                  於報告期間結束當日已使用、耗用或出
      disposed of as at the end of the reporting period.                                 售之授權使用存貨之價值。

      The licence agreement between the Licensor and the Licencee had                    授權人與獲授權人訂立的授權使用協議
      been expired on 1 July 2019 and the Licensor had not entered any                   已於二零一九年七月一日屆滿，授權人

35. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                     35. 資本風險管理
      The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the Group will     本集團管理其資本以確保通過優化債務
      be able to continue as a going concern while maximising the return to       與股本結存為權益持有人爭取最高回
      stakeholders through the optimisation of the debt and equity balance.       報，使本集團旗下實體能夠持續經營。
      The Group’s overall strategy remains unchanged from prior year.             本集團之整體策略與過往年度相同。

      The capital structure of the Group consists of bank borrowings, net         誠如綜合權益變動表所披露，本集團之
      of cash and cash equivalents and equity attributable to owners of           資本結構包括銀行借貸（扣除現金及現
      the Company, comprising issued share capital, other reserves and            金等值項目）及本公司持有人應佔權益
      retained profits as disclosed in the consolidated statement of changes      （包括已發行股本、其他儲備及保留溢
      in equity.                                                                  利）。

      The Directors review the capital structure on a semi-annual basis.          董事每半年審閱資本結構。作為審閱一
      As part of this review, the Directors consider the cost of capital and      部分，董事將考慮資本成本與各類資本
      the risks associated with each class of capital. In the opinion of the      的風險。董事認為，本集團將通過支付
      Directors, the Group will balance its overall capital structure through     股息、發行新股、購回股份、發行新債
      the payment of dividends, new share issues and share buy-backs as           或者贖回現有債項，以平衡整體資本結
      well as the issue of new debt or the redemption of existing debt.           構。
